Assessment**\n\n- Scope of Impact: This failure had a limited impact, with a peak message backlog of approximately 1,370 messages. It only caused minor alert triggering delays, and the overall platform service was not widely affected.\n- Business Impact: Affected users experienced delays in alert triggering, which may have resulted in delayed notifications and a certain risk of lagging incident response.\n- Duration: Approximately 115 minutes\n- Data Loss: None. All alerts were eventually triggered normally, with only delays and no data loss.\n\n**Root Cause**\n\nAn anomaly in the vector database for intelligent aggregation caused Search requests to be blocked for 60 seconds, rapidly exhausting the limited concurrency slots of the Kafka consumer. This led to a decrease in consumption throughput and continuous backlog in the alert_add topic.\n\n**Description of Existing Monitoring Mechanisms**\n\n- The system is configured with alert backlog monitoring, but the current alert threshold is set too high to trigger alerts in the early stage of the backlog.\n- The peak backlog in this incident was approximately 1,370 messages, which did not reach the existing alert threshold, causing the issue to go undetected initially. Additionally, backlogs are generally considered normal, as the service will continue processing after Kafka peak shaving until the backlog is fully cleared.\n\n**Future Improvements**\n\n**Business Logic:**\n\n- Add a query timeout for the vector database to reduce blocking time – to be completed within this week\n\n**Monitoring and Alerts:**\n\n- Lower the alert threshold: Adjust the message backlog alert threshold and add a low-threshold warning to ensure early detection of backlogs – to be completed within this week\n- Tiered alerts: Implement a multi-level alert strategy (Warning → Alert → Critical) to enable graded notifications and improve response efficiency – to be completed within this week\n- Vector database: Add latency monitoring alerts for Search operations – to be completed within this week\n- Response delay: Monitor the average latency of the Search interface and trigger alerts when it exceeds the threshold, enabling early detection of vector database performance degradation – to be completed within this week\n- Incident triggering delay: Based on the incident_triggered histogram, monitor the P90 of incidents and trigger alerts immediately when it exceeds the SLO threshold – to be completed within this week\n\n---\n\n**影响评估**\n\n- 影响范围：此次故障影响范围有限，消息堆积峰值仅约 1370 条，仅涉及少量的告警触发延迟，整体平台服务未受到大面积影响\n\n- 业务影响：受影响用户的告警触发出现延迟，可能导致无法及时收到通知，存在一定的故障响应滞后风险\n\n- 持续时间：约 115 分钟\n\n- 数据丢失：无，所有告警最终均已正常触发，仅存在延迟，无数据丢失\n\n**故障根因**\\\n智能聚合的向量数据库异常导致 Search 请求阻塞 60 秒，快速耗尽 Kafka consumer 的有限并发槽，引发消费吞吐降低，以及 alert_add topic 持续积压\n\n**现有监控机制说明**\n\n- 系统已配置消息堆积量告警，但现有告警阈值设置较高，未能在堆积早期阶段及时触发告警\n- 本次堆积峰值约 1370 条，未达到现有告警阈值，导致问题未被第一时间发现，另外积压一般属于正常现象，kafka 削峰后服务将持续处理，直至积压全部消化\n\n**后续改进**\\\n**业务逻辑：**\n\n- 增加向量数据库的查询超时时间，降低阻塞时间 - 本周内完成\n\n**监控预警：**\n\n- 降低告警阈值：调整消息堆积告警阈值，增加低阈值预警，确保在堆积早期即可感知 - 本周内完成\n\n- 分级告警：设置多级告警策略（预警 → 警告 → 严重），实现梯度告知，提升响应效率 - 本周内完成\n\n- 向量数据库： Search 操作增加耗时监控告警 - 本周内完成\n\n- 响应延迟：监控 Search 接口 Avg 耗时，超过阈值告警，提前感知向量库性能劣化 - 本周内完成\n\n- 故障触发延迟：基于 incident_triggered histogram，监控 Incident P90 ，超过 SLO 阈值即时告警 - 本周内完成"])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"26:T109c,"])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"**Impact Assessment**\n\n- Scope of Impact: This failure had a limited impact, with a peak message backlog of approximately 1,370 messages. All alerts were eventually triggered normally, with only delays and no data loss.\n\n**Root Cause**\n\nAn anomaly in the vector database for intelligent aggregation caused Search requests to be blocked for 60 seconds, rapidly exhausting the limited concurrency slots of the Kafka consumer. This led to a decrease in consumption throughput and continuous backlog in the alert_add topic.\n\n**Description of Existing Monitoring Mechanisms**\n\n- The system is configured with alert backlog monitoring, but the current alert threshold is set too high to trigger alerts in the early stage of the backlog.\n- The peak backlog in this incident was approximately 1,370 messages, which did not reach the existing alert threshold, causing the issue to go undetected initially. 本周内完成\n\n**监控预警：**\n\n- 降低告警阈值：调整消息堆积告警阈值，增加低阈值预警，确保在堆积早期即可感知 - 本周内完成\n\n- 分级告警：设置多级告警策略（预警 → 警告 → 严重），实现梯度告知，提升响应效率 - 本周内完成\n\n- 向量数据库： Search 操作增加耗时监控告警 - 本周内完成\n\n- 响应延迟：监控 Search 接口 Avg 耗时，超过阈值告警，提前感知向量库性能劣化 - 本周内完成\n\n- 故障触发延迟：基于 incident_triggered histogram，监控 Incident P90 ，超过 SLO 阈值即时告警 - 本周内完成"])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"27:T8c2,"])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"\n**Impact Scope**\n\n- Access to \u003chttps://api.flashcat.cloud\u003e from overseas regions, specifically affecting alert event pushes and OpenAPI calls.\n\n**Change Details**\n\n- **New Relay Gateway:** We are deploying a new Relay Gateway for overseas regions. All requests will be forwarded to our domestic infrastructure through this gateway to enhance connection quality.\n- **DNS Switch:** The DNS resolution for overseas lines will be switched from the original link to this new entry point.\n\n**Background**\n\n- This optimization addresses issues caused by cross-region back-to-origin traffic, such as long latency, high delay, timeouts, and connection interruptions (EOF). By introducing the Relay Gateway—equipped with circuit breaking, retry, and request queuing mechanisms—we can effectively shield underlying network instability and ensure the reliable delivery of overseas alerts.\n\n**Rollback Plan**\n\n- If necessary, we will revert the DNS resolution for overseas lines to the original link. The expected time to take effect is 1–10 minutes.\n\n**Note:** Please monitor the status of your overseas alert pushes and OpenAPI calls during this maintenance window. If you encounter any failed requests, please contact us immediately. Thank you for your support and understanding.\n\n---\n\n**影响范围：**\n\n- 境外区域访问 \u003chttps://api.flashcat.cloud\u003e ，涉及告警事件推送及 OpenAPI 调用\n\n**变更内容：**\n\n- 新增境外 Relay Gateway 请求转发网关，境外所有请求通过网关转发至国内，提升请求质量\n- 将境外线路的 DNS 解析，从原链路切换至新入口\n\n**变更背景：**\n\n- 针对境外客户告警推送需跨区域回源国内导致的链路冗长、高延迟及网络波动引发的超时与连接中断（EOF）问题，本次优化将引入Relay Gateway（中继网关）。该方案利用网关内置的熔断、重试及请求排队机制，有效屏蔽底层网络不稳定性，全方位保障境外告警推送的可靠投递。\n\n**回滚方案：**\n\n- 将境外线路的 DNS 解析切回原链路，预计 1\\~10 分钟生效。\n\n请您在变更期间关注境外的告警推送和OpenAPI调用情况，如有失败请求，请及时联系我们，感谢您的支持与理解。"])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"22:[\"$\",\"$L24\",null,{\"pageId\":5808782492839,\"initialDateView\":\"calendar\",\"initialCalendarData\":{\"changes\":[{\"change_id\":6467288037973,\"page_id\":5808782492839,\"type\":\"incident\",\"title\":\"部分邮件发送失败 | Partial Email Delivery Failure\",\"description\":\"我们已通过增加邮件推送节点完成修复，状态页邮件推送能力已恢复正常。对于本次故障期间给您造成的不便，以及部分邮件通知未能成功送达的情况，我们深表歉意。我们会继续优化邮件推送链路，降低类似问题再次发生的可能性。\\n\\n---\\n\\nWe have completed the fix by adding additional email delivery nodes, and status page email notifications have now been restored to normal. We sincerely apologize for the inconvenience caused during this incident, including any email notifications that were not delivered successfully. We will continue to improve our email delivery infrastructure to reduce the likelihood of similar issues recurring.\",\"status\":\"resolved\",\"affected_components\":[{\"component_id\":\"01KD7Q8JAYDXQ49HC0PG9VPA5P\",\"section_id\":\"01KD8E14SCZZ3QQTE63PA731GW\",\"name\":\"Notification Delivery\",\"description\":\"Delivery of incident and on-call notifications across multiple channels.\",\"available_since_seconds\":1672502400,\"order_id\":3,\"status\":\"operational\"}],\"start_at_seconds\":1779344280,\"close_at_seconds\":1779440335,\"is_retrospective\":true,\"updates\":[{\"update_id\":\"01KS4MPTB12ZMC87GBE1VXXPTW\",\"at_seconds\":1779344280,\"status\":\"investigating\",\"description\":\"我们发现部分状态页邮件推送未能成功发出，可能影响部分用户接收状态页相关的邮件通知。我们正在调查具体原因，并会在确认影响范围和根因后及时更新进展。\\n\\n---\\n\\nWe have identified that some status page email notifications were not sent successfully, which may affect some users receiving status page-related email notifications. We are currently investigating the cause and will provide further updates once the impact scope and root cause are confirmed.\",\"component_changes\":[{\"component_id\":\"01KD7Q8JAYDXQ49HC0PG9VPA5P\",\"component_name\":\"Notification Delivery\",\"status\":\"partial_outage\"}]},{\"update_id\":\"01KS4MPTB12ZMC87GBE4N2AH9Q\",\"at_seconds\":1779344400,\"status\":\"identified\",\"description\":\"由于 Gmail 服务的流控限制，Flashduty 部分通过 Gmail 发出的邮件未能成功发送。我们已经定位到本次故障的原因，并正在尝试修复，以尽快恢复邮件发送能力。在修复完成前，部分用户可能会遇到邮件通知延迟或未送达的情况。\\n\\n---\\n\\nDue to rate limiting imposed by the Gmail service, some emails sent through Gmail by Flashduty were not delivered successfully. We have identified the root cause of this incident and are working on a fix to restore email delivery as soon as possible. Until the fix is completed, some users may experience delayed or undelivered email notifications.\"},{\"update_id\":\"01KS6RNMDYXZP7QBQ68Z3691D1\",\"at_seconds\":1779417403,\"status\":\"monitoring\",\"description\":\"我们已采取修复措施，并正在持续观察状态页邮件推送的恢复情况。目前系统处于监控中，我们会继续确认邮件发送是否稳定，并在确认问题完全恢复后更新状态。\\n\\n---\\n\\nWe have applied mitigation measures and are continuously monitoring the recovery of status page email delivery. The system is currently under monitoring, and we will continue to verify whether email delivery remains stable. We will provide another update once the issue is fully resolved.\",\"component_changes\":[{\"component_id\":\"01KD7Q8JAYDXQ49HC0PG9VPA5P\",\"component_name\":\"Notification Delivery\",\"status\":\"degraded\"}]},{\"update_id\":\"01KS7EHEJYJWW0D8K23FWNS192\",\"at_seconds\":1779440335,\"status\":\"resolved\",\"description\":\"我们已通过增加邮件推送节点完成修复，状态页邮件推送能力已恢复正常。对于本次故障期间给您造成的不便，以及部分邮件通知未能成功送达的情况，我们深表歉意。我们会继续优化邮件推送链路，降低类似问题再次发生的可能性。\\n\\n---\\n\\nWe have completed the fix by adding additional email delivery nodes, and status page email notifications have now been restored to normal. We sincerely apologize for the inconvenience caused during this incident, including any email notifications that were not delivered successfully. We will continue to improve our email delivery infrastructure to reduce the likelihood of similar issues recurring.\",\"component_changes\":[{\"component_id\":\"01KD7Q8JAYDXQ49HC0PG9VPA5P\",\"component_name\":\"Notification Delivery\",\"status\":\"operational\"}]}],\"notify_subscribers\":true},{\"change_id\":6457927543973,\"page_id\":5808782492839,\"type\":\"incident\",\"title\":\"Incident Triggering Delay  ｜ 故障延迟触发\",\"description\":\"$25\",\"status\":\"resolved\",\"affected_components\":[{\"component_id\":\"01KD9MWXMKS0867Q777E0E1NZN\",\"section_id\":\"01KD8E14SCZZ3QQTE63PA731GW\",\"name\":\"Incident Response\",\"description\":\"Incident lifecycle management from creation to resolution.\",\"available_since_seconds\":1672502400,\"order_id\":2,\"status\":\"operational\"}],\"start_at_seconds\":1779066780,\"close_at_seconds\":1779073980,\"is_retrospective\":true,\"updates\":[{\"update_id\":\"01KRZ6BH325M075NBH7Z1MRZ02\",\"at_seconds\":1779066780,\"status\":\"investigating\",\"description\":\"The monitoring system is generating alerts normally, but there is intermittent triggering delay on the Flashduty side.\\n\\n---\\n\\n源监控系统正常产生告警，但在 Flashduty 侧出现间歇性触发延迟。\",\"component_changes\":[{\"component_id\":\"01KD9MWXMKS0867Q777E0E1NZN\",\"component_name\":\"Incident Response\",\"status\":\"degraded\"}]},{\"update_id\":\"01KRZ6BH325M075NBH80DEW010\",\"at_seconds\":1779073980,\"status\":\"resolved\",\"description\":\"$26\",\"component_changes\":[{\"component_id\":\"01KD9MWXMKS0867Q777E0E1NZN\",\"component_name\":\"Incident Response\",\"status\":\"operational\"}]}]},{\"change_id\":6333987562973,\"page_id\":5808782492839,\"type\":\"maintenance\",\"title\":\"Overseas Link Optimization Notice ｜ 境外链路优化通知\",\"description\":\"The current change has been completed.
